# Deep audit 1102: report

Pinned shas: claude-settings `da8d8073`, q_max `5bbf10e13`, banchi `ce3c0b09`, degen `22256a40`, joyous `53b5046`, mailaudit `cd3e12f`.

Plan: `docs/plans/deep-audit.md` on branch `deep-audit-plan`. Scripts: `docs/plans/deep-audit/run1/`.

## Method

- Run 1: `extract.py` listed 13,512 claims and 361 second-copy candidates. 86 Sonnet agents swept them and 11 open PRs. They returned 1,395 findings.
- Quote checks: `quotecheck.py` kept 1,304. `prcheck.py` re-read 89 dropped PR findings at their PR branch and restored 88. 1,392 went to Run 2.
- Run 2: `split.py` cut them into 39 chunks of up to 40. Each chunk had a judge and a refuter, then one write-up per repo. All Run 2 agents ran Sonnet (see Deviations).
- Survived: 1043 findings. Homes: 262 lanes, debts, notes and owner questions.

## Deviations from the plan

- Run 2 ran Sonnet, not Opus. The live setting `CLAUDE_CODE_WORKFLOW_SUBAGENT_MODEL=sonnet` overrides the script's `model: 'opus'`. The owner chose Sonnet over a settings raise (2026-11-03 12:07 CST).
- Run 2 judged chunks of 40, not one judge per topic, on the owner's pick (2026-11-03 10:32 CST). Cross-claim checks span one chunk, not a whole topic.
- Run 2 wrote one section per repo, so private-repo text stays in that repo's own branch.
- Judges gave some added findings a free-text topic. They count here as `other (judge-added)`.
- The harness rewrote `<` in 13 Run 1 outputs; 8 dropped findings had `<` in a quote, so they may be false drops.

## claude-settings

Point: the sweep's findings merge into 83 homes: 36 one-home lanes for duplicates, 28 other lanes, 1 debt, 10 notes and 8 owner questions. 28 findings rate medium harm. The rest are low. Main was read at da8d8073. Findings on open pull request branches name the branch. Six medium cites were re-read in this worktree (f5fda92b) and held (ci-scope.sh:55, protect.py:73, settings.json:11, stall_watch.py:25, merge.py:279, no-merge-onto-red-main.md:21). The rest rest on the sweep's verdict pass. I did not re-read them. Pull request state now is unknown for every branch finding.

Harm means what a session in normal work does with the wrong text, or an act a guard or check promises to stop and does not. Findings in q_max, degen and joyous are cited by file and line only. Their text is not quoted or described here.

### Read first, ranked by harm

1. Raw merge is allowed with no decision behind it (settings.json:11, docs/decisions/auto-mode-owner-acts.md:5). The red-main flag has two readings of who may type it (docs/decisions/no-merge-onto-red-main.md:21, merge/merge.py:279, :1910). [Private text removed; names joyous. Cites: CLAUDE.md:30-33.] [Private text removed; names joyous. Cites: none.]
2. The guard for discarded output covers seven git writes only (hooks/guard.py:437, lint/rule_mechanisms.json:242-247, docs/plans/refusal-vs-rule.md:46). Any other command with its output sent to /dev/null passes. Home: owner question output-discard-rule-scope.
3. A pull request that edits actions/playwright-setup skips the checks-guard run that uses it (.github/scripts/ci-scope.sh:54-55, .github/workflows/checks.yml:578). The break shows only on main. Home: lane ci-scope-playwright-action.
4. Texts that steer sessions into a refusal. [Private text removed; names degen, q_max. Cites: docs/plans/next-waves.md:29, specs/briefs/wave13.md:34, wave14.md:27, docs/specs/plan.md:2476, docs/decisions/ci-wait-reads-run-status.md:5.] The project-ops template, skill and README still carry usage limits that session-tiers.md:18 ruled out (skills/project-ops/templates/operating-model.md:70, skills/project-ops/SKILL.md:17, README.md:289). Homes: lanes qmax-gh-run-watch-text [Private text removed; names degen. Cites: none.]
5. Promised checks that do not run. claude-md-cites runs in no child CI (docs/decisions/child-cites-not-copies.md:15). The compliance probe reads no owner grant (docs/decisions/compliance-defined.md:10, lint/compliance_probes.py:505-516). The reply judge is no backstop for a widening option in a page write (docs/decisions/uniform-over-variants.md:14). Homes: debt claude-md-cites-not-wired, lane exemption-grant-unread, owner question widening-backstop-gap.
6. Agent files miss lines the record says every agent has. The staged-diff step is missing from agents/designer.md:82 and agents/record-writer.md:20 (docs/decisions/lanes-start-in-tool-worktrees.md:37). TaskStop is missing from agents/design-reviewer.md and agents/claude-code-guide.md (docs/debts/agent-reports-with-live-watcher.md:3). Homes: lanes staged-diff-step-agents, taskstop-all-agents.
7. On this Windows machine install.ps1 overwrites a plain settings.json with no backup (install.ps1:985-1000, docs/decisions/copy-mode-overwrites-no-backup.md:5). Home: lane install-ps1-backup.
8. The stall watcher polls on a 0.2 s timer for each background job (hooks/stall_watch.py:25-26, docs/decisions/stall-watch.md:65). CLAUDE.md:11 forbids that, and the record says it adds no timer. Home: owner question stall-watch-timer-rule.
9. banchi runs a vendored STE lint that has drifted from the source (banchi scripts/ste/ste_lint.py:2 against lint/ste_lint.py:128). Home: lane banchi-ste-lint-one-home.
10. protect.py sets enforce_admins false on new repos (skills/project-ops/scripts/protect.py:73). The gold form requires true (docs/decisions/main-branch-protection.md:23, :41). Home: lane protect-enforce-admins.

---

## banchi

One live code defect, about a dozen medium records that would mislead a session, and a long low tail of stale text and hand copies. One habit causes most of it: a ruling changes, and the old record is appended to, not rewritten in place (decisions README:9 says rewrite in place). Hand-typed counts have no reader. Copied constants have no compare check. I merged repeated rows and kept the higher harm. Where verification narrowed a row, I report the narrowed claim. I did not tally the input rows. Output: 50 lanes (24 are one-home lanes for PR #1106, the one-home check build in claude-settings), 2 debts, 7 owner questions, 1 note.

Cite form. Unmarked cites are banchi. `D<n>:<line>` means `docs/decisions/D<n>-*.md` at that line. Cites marked (claude-settings) are in that repo. A backtick slug is a lane in the structured list.

### Contradictions

**High**
- Archive sweep. D222 (archive sweep paces) says a block raises "answered HTTP 403" (D222:25). The code raises the 'forbidden' text (pipeline/pricehistory.py:1044-1046). So BLOCKED_SIGNATURE (pipeline/pricearchive.py:127) never matches, blocked reads 0, and the throttle branches (cli/cmd_pricearchive.py:303-335) never run. cli/archive_review.py:71-95 (signatures at :79) then queues a host 403 as a photo fault. D238 (archive refusals go to review):9 repeats it. The self-test hand-writes the old text (scripts/pricearchive-selftest.py:1675), so it cannot see the drift. Lane `block-signature-drift`.

**Medium: a session that follows the record builds or trusts the wrong thing**
- CI rules. D141 (browser matrix when reachable):68 says a draft fails the rollup. D136 (suite sharded, never widened):62 and .github/workflows/check.yml:338 say a draft skips every job. D141:19 and check.yml:525 name a job-level `if:` that check.yml:563 replaced. D161 (check product before guards):36 names a required job `check`; check.yml has none (:336 is all-jobs-passed), so a branch-protection setup from D161 would block every merge. Lane `ci-records-rewrite`. Lows in that lane: D136:38 vs D136:13 (three vs twelve shards); D136:50 vs D141:11 (billing: unmeasured here); D118 (press never moves content):77 vs check.yml:538, and check.yml:708 (one prong survives); D161:15 vs D161:3 and :24 (one prong survives); D161:26 vs Makefile:441; D215 (CI runs reachable specs):5 vs app/tests/routeSweep.ts:21; D018 (generators write, never gate):25 vs check.yml:14; D140 (merge claims the decision number):25 vs check.yml:165; D161:13, D122 (suite takes machine lock):80 and Makefile:457 say nine tests, harness/run.py:61-72 and CLAUDE.md:45 say ten.

---

## mailaudit

Rows with the same claim are merged. Each line is one finding with one home in brackets. Lines run from high harm to low within each kind. Paths are in mailaudit unless a line names another repo. The one private-repo cite is degen, by file and line only, with no text.

Homes: 26 lanes (15 with one_home true; they feed the one-home check in claude-settings PR #1106), 4 owner questions, 2 debts, 2 notes. Every verdict rests on a read of mailaudit at cd3e12f. Nothing was run live. A runtime claim is marked unmeasured where a line says so.

### Contradictions

High
- mailaudit:docs/decisions/native-files-are-frozen.md:11 says no native build shipped. mailaudit:docs/decisions/native-ships-via-testflight.md:39 logs the install of 2026-10-22, and :43 says the names freeze then. A session could rename the ledger files and orphan the phone's data. [lane native-shipped-facts]

Medium
- mailaudit:docs/decisions/orchestrator-may-merge.md:5 gives merges to the coordinator. claude-settings:docs/decisions/standing-merge-grant.md:7 (owner, 2026-11-01 21:34 CST) says the coordinator never merges. [owner question merge-holder-ruling]
- mailaudit:docs/decisions/native-swift-rebuild.md:80 rejects iPhone-only and :34, :35, :68-70 promise one iOS and macOS target. mailaudit:native/project.yml (line unknown) builds iOS only. mailaudit:docs/decisions/native-build-lanes.md:63 and mailaudit:docs/decisions/phone-nav-is-bottom-bar.md:50 call iPhone-only a default that swift-rebuild supports. [owner question iphone-only-vs-mac]
- mailaudit:docs/decisions/recent-checks-can-be-undone.md:145 says the Recent cell shows when the ledger holds data, as Reset does. mailaudit:src/app.jsx:6573 and mailaudit:docs/decisions/recovery-control-not-state-gated.md:14 say Reset shows on every ledger. The same record at :56 states a paper fill as settled. mailaudit:docs/decisions/ruled-head-panels-carry-no-fill.md:3 calls it an unbuilt default. [owner question recent-cell-gate]
- GitHub token cluster. mailaudit:docs/decisions/ledger-private-source-public.md:5 (open) and mailaudit:docs/decisions/fine-grained-token-single-repo.md:3 tell a reader to put a GitHub token on each device. mailaudit:docs/decisions/live-verification.md:13 says no device holds one. mailaudit:CLAUDE.md:98 lists the token record without the Retired prefix. mailaudit:docs/decisions/token-expires-without-warning.md:9 cites a code comment that src does not hold. mailaudit:docs/decisions/older-versions-come-off-branch.md:3 reads History through a token (mailaudit:src/entry.jsx:512, :532). mailaudit:docs/decisions/pull-needs-a-key-too.md:5 says a mirror pull and push run, and :9 names a doMerge call that does not exist (mailaudit:src/app.jsx:4380, :4383). mailaudit:docs/decisions/itp-may-clear-storage.md:5 names a manual push as the fix, and mailaudit:docs/decisions/ledger-tab-holds-options.md:42 removed it. [owner question github-era-records-retire]
- Sha and mirror cluster. mailaudit:CLAUDE.md:84 and mailaudit:docs/decisions/sha-accepted-only-after-apply.md:5 say app.jsx calls acceptPull after applyBackup. acceptPull sits only at mailaudit:src/entry.jsx:589 and :596. mailaudit:docs/decisions/null-remote-info-is-not-no-key.md:7 names a variable that syncBroken never reads (mailaudit:src/app.jsx:3428-3442). mailaudit:docs/decisions/testing-mock-field-stops-decor.md:3 says syncedAt reads pulledAt, but app.jsx:3426 reads workerSyncedAt. mailaudit:docs/decisions/the-write-budget-is-shared.md:12 points at a retired GitHub budget (mailaudit:docs/decisions/push-and-merge-guard-differently.md:3). mailaudit:docs/decisions/sync-revamp-cloudflare.md:109 calls sync-runs-itself retired, but its Worker POST rule is live (mailaudit:docs/decisions/sync-runs-itself.md:7, :9). mailaudit:CLAUDE.md:167 says records hold current text only, but a-restore-is-not-local.md:17 and eight more records carry dated Retired lines. - Notes: test-id-reuse-37-53, raw-json-pin-thin.

Rulings: shared-tokens-and-fixtures.md; building-one-home-per-capability. Check: npm run check:screens passes; no colour literal for the card remains in test/screens.mjs. |
| test-id-reuse-37-53 | note |  | Note, weak. merge-later-change-wins.md:28 says test 37.53 (pull replaces stamps in full) was retired; test/app.test.mjs:3374 and :3414 say the same, but :3392 still carries the id 37.53 on a live History-restore test. | raw-json-pin-thin | note |  | Note. native-ledger-is-raw-json.md:23 says a top level of null, a number, a string or a list is refused with no kept copy, and :66 names testNonObjectTopLevelRefusesWithNoCopy as the pin. That test loops null, 42 and a string only (LedgerStoreTests.swift:546); the list case is in the failed-reload test (:562-566), which does not call keptCopies(). Behaviour holds; the pin is thinner than the record says. One line in the notes file. |
